# gameplay: pop_factions

Stellaris Cygnus v4.5.2 (9776). Extracted 2026-10-12. 895 entries. Original raw strings retained.

## pft_supremacist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_supremacist&lang=en)

### 中文

至高主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:10`

### English

Supremacist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:13`

## pft_supremacist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_supremacist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

至高派旨在保证国家创始物种的统治地位。它们推行利于本族的政策，而罔顾其他物种的利益。\n\n§L排外主义和军国主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

Supremacist Factions wish for their Empire's founding species to dominate. They push for policies that benefit their own species and disadvantage others.\n\n§LXenophobe & Militarist Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:14`

## pft_traditionalist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_traditionalist&lang=en)

### 中文

传统主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:12`

### English

Traditionalist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:15`

## pft_traditionalist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_traditionalist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传统派希望让帝国的思潮回归到步入星辰大海之前的传统道路上。他们宣扬虔诚苦修，蔑视激进科技和非唯心主义带来的各种社会变革。\n\n§L唯心主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

Traditionalist Factions desire a return to traditional values that may or may not have existed before the Empire took to the stars. They preach for pious austerity, and scorn the kinds of societal change brought about by radical technology and non-spiritualist values.\n\n§LSpiritualist Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:16`

## pft_progressive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_progressive&lang=en)

### 中文

进步主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

Progressive Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:17`

## pft_progressive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_progressive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进步派在现有的社会结构中争取公平。他们反对缺乏代表性的政府形式，并重视与其他帝国的平等合作。\n\n§L平等主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

Progressive Factions campaign for equity within existing social structures. They oppose non-representative forms of government, and value equal collaboration with other Empires.\n\n§LEgalitarian Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:18`

## pft_imperialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_imperialist&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:16`

### English

Imperialist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:19`

## pft_imperialist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_imperialist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国主义派渴望稳坐江山，同时统治其他国家。他们乐于与其他国家树敌并将其征服，厌恶依靠其他国家。\n\n§L军国主义和威权主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

Imperialist Factions crave dominance within our own borders, and over other Empires. They thrive on Rivalries and conquering other Empires, and dislike relying on others.\n\n§LMilitarist & Authoritarian Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:20`

## pft_isolationist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_isolationist&lang=en)

### 中文

孤立主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

Isolationist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:21`

## pft_isolationist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_isolationist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

孤立主义派倾向于把广泛的星系排除在内部事务之外。他们把与其他帝国的对话保持在最低限度，以避免侵略战争（但并非自废武装）。\n\n§L和平主义和排外主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

Isolationist Factions prefer when the wider galaxy is kept out of our internal affairs. They campaign to keep discourse with other Empires at a bare minimum, eschewing wars of aggression - but not at the cost of our becoming an easy target.\n\n§LXenophobe & Pacifist Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:22`

## pft_technologist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_technologist&lang=en)

### 中文

技术主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:20`

### English

Technologist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:23`

## pft_technologist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_technologist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

技术派主张在全国范围采取鼓励科学发展的政策，以利于取得大规模的技术进步。他们厌恶由于政治因素而对某些科研活动颁布禁令，但支持签订科研协议。他们喜欢从技术上超越别的国家，讨厌被其他国家超越。技术派对于人工智能技术的推广运用乐见其成。\n\n§L唯物主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

Technologist Factions desire an Empire-wide embrace of scientific progress and the fruits of technological advancement. They dislike politically-motivated bans on certain avenues of research, as well as being outpaced by other Empires.\n\n§LMaterialist Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:24`

## pft_totalitarian

极权派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

Totalitarian Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:25`

## pft_totalitarian_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_totalitarian_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

极权派梦想建立一个等级森严的社会，由一位强权人物或一个专制集团统治。\n\n§L威权主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

Totalitarian Factions dream of a strictly stratified society led by an single authority figure or cabal.\n\n§LAuthoritarian Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:26`

## pft_xenoist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_xenoist&lang=en)

### 中文

亲外派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:24`

### English

Xenoist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:27`

## pft_xenoist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_xenoist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲外派推崇外星生命和文化。他们热衷于人口多元化和与其他物种的互动。他们反对物种层面的奴役和清洗。\n\n§L亲外主义和平等主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

Xenoist Factions champion alien life and culture. They enjoy diverse populations and interactions with other species. They oppose species-wide Slavery and Purges.\n\n§LXenophile & Egalitarian Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:28`

## pft_prosperity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_prosperity&lang=en)

### 中文

繁荣主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:26`

### English

Prosperity Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:29`

## pft_prosperity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_prosperity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

繁荣派希望国家经济能够持续增长并且资源丰收。他们推行自由贸易，不希望爆发影响国内发展的武装冲突。\n\n§L和平主义和亲外主义思潮§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

Prosperity Factions desire economic growth and the acquisition of resources. They campaign for the free flow of trade, and they dislike the kinds of armed conflicts that disrupt internal development.\n\n§LPacifist & Xenophile Ethos§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:30`

## pft_democratic_election_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_democratic_election_1&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:31`

## pft_democratic_election_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_democratic_election_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

民主派在国家领导人选举期间出现，随后在人群中迅速发展，它们致力于把那些受民众欢迎的领袖推上政坛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

Democratic Party Factions emerge during Empire Elections and and dissipate shortly thereafter, serving as a means to put certain popular Leaders in the political limelight.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:32`

## SUPREMACIST_RULER_NOT_SAME_SPECIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASUPREMACIST_RULER_NOT_SAME_SPECIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]希望统治者是该物种的成员，任何非[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]担任[Root.Owner.GetRulerTitle]都会惹怒他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

The [Root.GetName] want our Ruler to be of their species and will be angered by any non-[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] holding the position of [Root.Owner.GetRulerTitle].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:43`

## ISOLATIONIST_NO_DIPLO_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AISOLATIONIST_NO_DIPLO_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

如果不能撤销与外国势力间不必要的外交联系——比如移民协议，科研协议以及防御条约——这将会遭致[Root.GetName]的厌恶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Failing to eliminate non-essential ties to foreign powers - such as Migration Treaties, Research Agreements, and Defensive Pacts - will displease the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:272`

## TRADITIONALIST_UNBLISSED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATRADITIONALIST_UNBLISSED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们的人民保持清醒，使得他们随时都能够认识到他们肩上的责任与义务。任何我国人民均不处于化学极乐生活标准下将可以取悦[Root.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

Keeping the populace aware and cognizant of their duties and responsibilities by subjecting none of them to the Chemical Bliss living standard will please the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:347`

## pft_sector_separatists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_sector_separatists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星域分裂主义者可能会出现在星域的各个星球里，意在让邻近的星球脱离帝国统治。如果有了足够的民众支持，他们就有可能发起武装革命。\n\n§H星域分裂主义者绝不会出现在我们的核心星域里。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Sector Separatists may appear on worlds in sectors, seeking to free their stellar neighborhood from imperial rule. Given enough popular Support, they may even take up arms.\n\n§HSector Separatists will never appear in our core sector.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:629`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

Repatriation Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:641`

## pft_nationalist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_nationalist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

回归派系的人们试图将自己从我们的国家中以独立国家身份被释放出去，这样他们就能自主重建他们所称颂的国家。如果国内其他的人群发现他们独立后的统治比我们的更诱人，甚至连其他人口也有可能会加入他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

Pops in Repatriation Factions seek to be released from our Empire as independent nations, so they can be re-integrated by the Empire they hail from. Other Pops may also join them, if they find foreign rule more enticing than ours.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:642`

## POP_FACTION_NATIONALIST_DEMAND_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APOP_FACTION_NATIONALIST_DEMAND_TITLE&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

## docile_slaves_attraction_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adocile_slaves_attraction_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被奴役的人口一般都属于一个奴隶派系，无智慧的机器人人口除外。其他人口不会加入这个派系。\n\n§H这一数值显示被奴役人口成为温顺奴隶的吸引力。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

Enslaved Pops will always belong to a Slave Faction, excepting non-sapient robotic Pops. No other Pops will join.\n\n§HThis value shows enslaved Pops' attraction to the Docile Slaves.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:670`

## malcontent_slaves_attraction_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amalcontent_slaves_attraction_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被奴役的人口一般都属于一个奴隶派系，无智慧的机器人人口除外。其他人口不会加入这个派系。\n\n§H这一数值显示被奴役人口成为反叛奴隶的吸引力。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Enslaved Pops will always belong to a Slave Faction, excepting non-sapient robotic Pops. No other Pops will join.\n\n§HThis value shows enslaved Pops' attraction to the Malcontent Slaves.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:672`

## purged_attraction_desc

### 中文

除了机器人人口，被净化的人口总是归属于反净化派系。\n\n其他人口不会加入。

## pft_slave

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_slave&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

Slave Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:678`

## pft_slave_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_slave_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶派系由帝国中的奴隶人口组成。他们的主要诉求是获得解放。\n\n§H所有奴隶人口会自动加入该派系。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

The Slave Faction is made up of our Empire's enslaved Pops. Their primary demand is to be emancipated.\n\n§HAll Enslaved Pops automatically join this Faction.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:679`

## pft_docile_slaves

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_docile_slaves&lang=en)

### 中文

温驯奴隶派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

Docile Slaves Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:681`

## pft_docile_slaves_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_docile_slaves_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国被奴隶人口中的部分乃至全部奴隶都是属于温驯奴隶派系。他们不爱招惹事端，只希望能够提升工作环境。\n\n§H所有的奴隶都隶属于温驯奴隶派系或不满奴隶派系。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

The Docile Slaves Faction is made up of part or all of our Empire's enslaved Pops. They are generally obedient and only wish for improved working conditions.\n\n§HAll slaves belong to either the Docile Slaves or Malcontent Slaves.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:682`

## pft_malcontent_slaves

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_malcontent_slaves&lang=en)

### 中文

不满奴隶派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

Malcontent Slaves Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:684`

## pft_malcontent_slaves_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_malcontent_slaves_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国被奴隶人口中的部分乃至全部奴隶都是属于反叛奴隶派系。他们不愿伺服，时刻都在追求自由生活。\n\n§H所有的奴隶都隶属于温驯奴隶派系或不满奴隶派系。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

The Malcontent Slaves Faction is made up of part or all of our Empire's enslaved Pops. They are rebellious and seek emancipation.\n\n§HAll slaves belong to either the Docile Slaves or Malcontent Slaves.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:685`

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

An Anti-Purge Faction is made up of all the Pops our Empire is currently Purging as well as those sympathetic to their plight. Their primary demand is to be allowed to live.\n\n§HAll Pops being Purged automatically join this Faction.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:754`

## factions.1.title

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:830`

## factions.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个新的派系正在[Root.Owner.GetName]的政治圈中逐渐壮大。他们自称[Root.GetName]，其成员正紧密敦促我们处理他们关心的诸多诉求。\n\n可以预见，随着人口的增长和思潮的分化，各种派系都将如雨后春笋般冒出。要求得不到满足的派系会成为麻烦的源头，而支持帝国工作的派系可以为我们所用……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

A new faction has recently been gaining traction in the internal political landscape of the [Root.Owner.GetName]. They call themselves the [Root.GetName], and their members have been pushing hard for us to deal with the various issues that concern them.\n\nWe are likely to see the emergence of additional factions in the future, as our population grows and their ethics drift in different directions. A disgruntled faction will be a source of trouble, but those that approve of the government's actions could prove useful...

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:831`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.start&lang=en)

一个新的派系正在[Root.Owner.GetName]的政界中逐渐壮大。他们自称§H[Root.GetName]§!。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

A new faction has recently been gaining traction in the internal political landscape of the [Root.Owner.GetName]. They call themselves the §H[Root.GetName]§!. 

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:832`

## factions.1.desc.imperialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.imperialist&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的成员致力于确保我们对星系其他区域的军事统治。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Their members have been pushing hard for us to assert our military dominance over the rest of the galaxy.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:836`

## factions.1.desc.isolationist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.isolationist&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的成员倡议关闭边境并且对星系事务保持不干涉原则。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

Their members are advocating for closed borders and non-interference with galactic affairs.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:837`

## factions.1.desc.progressive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.progressive&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

Their members work for equality and justice for all denizens of our empire.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:838`

## factions.1.desc.prosperity

### 中文

他们的成员致力于让国家专注于通过和平的手段来发展经济与维护社会稳定。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_imperialist

他们的成员致力于使用我们武器库中所有强大的强化体武器来统治星系的其余部分。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_isolationist

### English

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:858`

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_progressive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.unplugged_progressive&lang=en)

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_prosperity

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_supremacist

## factions.1.desc.unplugged_totalitarian

## factions.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

既有派系和新兴运动都对我们在§Y[root.GetName]§!建立的新殖民地产生了异常浓烈的兴趣。从我们宣布在这个适宜的[root.GetPlanetHabitat]定居的那一刻起，[root.owner.GetName]内的几乎所有政权都对此有话要说。\n\n我们的分析人士认为没有什么重大变故正在[root.GetName]酝酿，但还是建议小心谨慎。显然，我们的各种政治实体打算就着这个新兴世界大肆宣传。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

Established Factions and fledgling movements alike have taken an unusually keen interest in our new colony on §Y[root.GetName]§!. From the moment we announced settlement of this modest [root.GetPlanetHabitat], nearly every political voice in the [root.owner.GetName] has had something to say about it.\n\nOur analysts don't suspect that anything truly radical is taking root on [root.GetName], but they advise some caution. It's clear our various political entities intend to campaign vigorously on this burgeoning world.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:893`

## planet_political_frontier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_political_frontier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这片殖民地被各种政治派系视为开展运动的沃土。

This colony is considered fertile ground for campaigns by Factions across the political spectrum.

## factions.3005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

报告：有关[root.GetName]的政论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

Report: Political Discourse on [root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:905`

## factions.3005.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

按照指示，我们在[root.GetName]的线人一直在定期汇报殖民地政治局势的最新进展。简而言之：前景向好。\n\n地方执法者和政治代理人设法用最少的资源把公开异见控制在了最低限度。尤其是流氓派系，没能在殖民者那里占到一丁点好处。结果是我们仍然牢牢地控制着政治生态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

As instructed, our informants on [root.GetName] have been delivering regular updates on the colony's developing political situation. In short: the trending outlook appears good.\n\nLocal enforcers and political agents have managed to keep outright dissent to a minimum, using only the bare minimum of resources. Rogue Factions in particular have struggled to gain any meaningful ground amongst the colonists; the result is a political environment which remains firmly within our control.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:906`

## factions.3005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

应当赞许这些爱国者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

These patriots are to be commended.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:907`

## factions.3005.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直在收集有关[root.GetName]政治局势的数据，证实了我们的许多担忧。激进的边缘性运动已经成功地在殖民地的人口中获得支持。这些公民已经被社会公平和流动性的说教所动摇，并且不再认同我们社会的一些基本原则。\n\n尽管场面不太好看，但这仍然是个好消息。我们已经积累了大量的研究，对于完善我们的[owner.GetAdj]殖民政治理论非常宝贵。此外，我们诱导了这些边缘因素，让它们在首都站稳脚跟前就自己暴露了出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

The data we've been gathering about the political situation on [root.GetName] lends credence to many of our concerns. Fringe campaigns of a radical nature have managed to drum up support amongst the colony's population. These citizens have been swayed by sermons of social equity and mobility, and no longer subscribe to some of the founding principles of our society.\n\nDespite appearances though, this still amounts to good news. We've amassed a substantial body of research, invaluable for refining our theories about [owner.GetAdj] colonial politics. Moreover: by luring out such fringe elements, we have exposed these threats long before they could ever gain a foothold on the capital.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:908`

## factions.3005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

或许……也许这种想法有一定可取之处？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

Or... perhaps there is some merit to this thinking?

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:909`

## planet_political_admittance

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_political_admittance&lang=en)

### 中文

宽松的政治

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

Permissive Politics

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:910`

## planet_political_admittance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_political_admittance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个殖民地批准了更大胆的政论形式，换取了更好的监管，加深了社会学理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

This colony permits more disruptive forms of political discourse in exchange for better monitoring and sociological understanding.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:911`

## factions.3005.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直在收集有关[root.GetName]政治运动的信息，得出了一些有趣的展望。我们发现当地对新思想的包容度正在提升（超出了我们先前认定的稳定的政治思想），但没有离经叛道之举。\n\n尽管任何偏离我们核心思想的行为都可以说是削弱了我们的权力基础，但这仍然能算是个好消息。这次试验相当于一个实际的研究样本，对于我们完善殖民政治理论非常宝贵。此外，我们已经证明，即便给予边缘因素一定的政治土壤，[owner.GetAdj]的人民也会批驳他们当中最激进的成分，并相对地向我们国家的原则靠拢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

The information we've been gathering about political campaigns on [root.GetName] has led to some interesting forecasts. We see a trend towards broader acceptance of new ideas - outside the bounds of what we would consider stable political thought - but no significant move towards radicalization.\n\nThough any deviation from our core message could be said to weaken our power base, this may still amount to good news. This experiment amounts to a substantial body of research, invaluable as we refine our theories about colonial politics. Moreover: we have proven that even when fringe elements are given some ground, the [owner.GetAdj] people refute their most radical elements, and stay relatively close to our nation's principles.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:912`

## factions.3005.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3005.c&lang=en)

### 中文

那么，这种行为也许是值得鼓励的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

Perhaps this conduct is to be encouraged, then.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:913`

## factions.3010.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的政治学家一直抱着热烈的兴趣观测着[root.GetName]。然而，尽管近期掀起了激烈的竞选活动，媒体还对此大肆渲染，他们仍预计该殖民地的政治走向会是相当典型的结果。\n\n我们的研究人员注意到，这里“没有消息”就等于是好消息：尽管我们社会中的一些边缘分子发表了声明，但目前还没有迹象表明[root.GetName]正在被塑造成独裁的温床。目前，民主仍然是这里的一项基础性原则。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:834`

### English

Our political scientists have been observing [root.GetName] with keen interest. However, despite feverish campaigning and a lot of recent fuss in the media, they forecast a fairly typical outcome for the colony's political future.\n\nOur researchers note that 'no news' amounts to good news here: despite what some fringe elements in our society may claim, there are as yet no signs that [root.GetName] is being molded into a hotbed of autocracy. Democracy remains a founding principle there, for now.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:915`

## factions.3010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

或许我们应该进行干涉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

Perhaps we ought to shake things up.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:916`

## factions.3010.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎我们社会之中的一些威权主义政治组织在[root.GetName]上生根发芽。他们的许多游说活动都把目标对准了这个蓬勃发展的殖民地；此外，尽管他们明显的成功尚未引起人们的警醒：我们的政治学家却认为需要对此抱持谨慎态度。对于[root.GetName]的[root.owner.GetAdj]而言，民主并非如同我们一开始想象的那样，是那么的重要或具有吸引力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

It seems that some of the more authoritarian political bodies in our society have gained a foothold on [root.GetName]. Their campaigns were amongst many targeting this burgeoning colony, and although their apparent success isn't cause for alarm in itself: our political scientists believe some caution may be warranted. Democracy may not be as essential or as appealing to the [root.owner.GetAdj] of [root.GetName] as we first thought.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:917`

## factions.3010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

对于我们的治国之道，这是颇有价值的检验！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

A worthy test for our way of governance!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:918`

## factions.3010.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须保护民主制度远离这类威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

We must guard against threats to democracy.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:919`

## factions.3010.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的政治学家对[root.GetName]进行了一段时间的监控，他们的报告证实当地存在着多种政治主张。\n\n目前还没有迹象表明[root.GetName]会陷入无政府状态，也没有类似模式的极端治理方式。然而，这种政治冷漠行为确实为[root.owner.GetAdj]的民主带来了一些独特的挑战——以及潜在风险，如果更极端的派系在此扎根的话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Our political scientists have been monitoring [root.GetName] for some time, and their reports confirm a wide range of political opinions have taken root there.\n\nThere are no signs yet that [root.GetName] will descend into anarchy, or other similarly extreme modes of governance. However, this much political jockeying does still present [root.owner.GetAdj] democracy with some unique challenges - and potential risks, if more extreme factions manage to gain a foothold there.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:920`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.3010.frontier.tt&lang=en)

§Y$planet_political_frontier$§!修正将会持续大约§Y9§!年，提高$MOD_POP_ETHICS_SHIFT_SPEED$§G+50%§!。

The §Y$planet_political_frontier$§! modifier will remain for approximately §Y9§! years, increasing $MOD_POP_ETHICS_SHIFT_SPEED$ by §G+50%§!.

效忠派对[Root.Owner.GetName]的[Root.Owner.GetRulerTitle][Root.Owner.GetRulerName]忠心耿耿，没有任何诉求。

## faction.6.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.6.title&lang=en)

### 中文

绝食抗议！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Hunger Strike!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:929`

## faction.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetName]的奴隶们通过自愿绝食，以毁掉自己身体的方式来对我们的生产进行蓄意破坏。[Root.GetName]派系的成员正在对[Root.Owner.GetName]内他们自己的人执行§Y净化§!。\n\n如果这一情况长期不得到重视，我们很有可能损失很大一部分的奴役人口。我们可以坐视这些可怜虫走向灭亡，也可以采用一些手段来处理这些不听话的派系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

The slaves of the [Root.Owner.GetName] have organized to sabotage our means of production - their bodies - through willing starvation. The [Root.GetName] faction members are effectively §YPurging§! themselves from the [Root.Owner.GetName].\n\nIf this situation goes unchecked for long, we risk losing a significant portion of our enslaved pops. We can either let the wretches die, or find some other way of dealing with this rogue faction.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:930`

## faction.7.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.7.title&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶起义！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

## faction.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[slave_planet.GetName]的奴隶对其主人发动了起义，他们组织了一支反抗军来破坏[Root.Owner.GetName]对他们的合法统治！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

The slaves of [slave_planet.GetName] have risen up against their masters, forming an illegal rebel coalition intending to break the [Root.Owner.GetName]'s lawful hold on them!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:933`

## faction.8.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.8.title&lang=en)

强制喂食

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Force Feeding

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:935`

## faction.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶的身体不属于他们自己，他们无权将之损害或扣留。\n\n我们结束了这场净化，而那些还活着的奴隶则得到了适当的营养补充。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

The body of a slave is not theirs to damage or withhold.\n\nTheir careless purge is ended, and all affected who still live have been dosed with appropriate amounts of nutrients.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:936`

## faction.10.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.10.title&lang=en)

奴隶不满！

### English

Slave Unrest!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:938`

## faction.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.10.desc&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

一股不满的情绪在[Root.Owner.GetName]的奴隶之间扩散开来，其中心是[unrest_planet.GetName]的[unrest_planet.GetPlanetMoon]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

A queasiness spreads within the enslaved aspects of the [Root.Owner.GetName], centered on the [unrest_planet.GetPlanetMoon] of [unrest_planet.GetName].

## faction.10.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.10.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[unrest_planet.GetName]§!的§H被奴役的人口§!，获得了§R奴隶骚动§!修正，持续§Y4年§!。

## faction.15.title

### 中文

奴隶暴徒！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

Slave Violence!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:943`

## faction.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一群暴徒——由擅离职守的奴隶组成——和[roving_gangs_planet.GetName]上的其他平民产生了激烈的冲突，并导致100口不幸逝世。\n\n死者的家属对这一事件的反应仍待观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

Roving mobs - comprised of derelict slaves - have gotten into violent confrontations with other members of the populace on [roving_gangs_planet.GetName], resulting in the untimely death of 100 Pops.\n\nIt remains to be seen how the associates of the deceased will react to this incident.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:944`

## faction.15.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.15.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[roving_gangs_planet]：\n§R100§!£pop£§H人口§!死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

[roving_gangs_planet]:\n§R100§! £pop£ §HPops§! die.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:945`

## faction.50.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.50.title&lang=en)

### 中文

星际地下铁路！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Interstellar Railroad!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:947`

## faction.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

显然，在我们的奴隶之中，几个激进的团体联合在了一起。他们的目的是将奴隶偷渡到[Root.Owner.GetAdj]领土之外。\n\n虽然尚且还不清楚这么大规模的犯罪到底是如何进行的，但可以确定的是，现在已经有奴隶逍遥于[Root.Owner.GetAdj]势力之外了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

It appears that some of the more radical elements in our slave population have banded together to, essentially, remove slaves from [Root.Owner.GetAdj] territories.\n\nWe do not know how a crime on this scale was committed, but we are fairly certain that the slaves are now free, somewhere outside of [Root.Owner.GetAdj] space.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:948`

## faction.50.desc.colle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.50.desc.colle&lang=en)

### 中文

看来，某个误入歧途的小派系十分热衷于通过将奴隶从自身的命运中解放出来并偷渡到[Root.Owner.GetAdj]边界之外让[Root.Owner.GetName]感到难堪。\n\n这些伤口对我们来说无足轻重，但我们希望那些迷途的羔羊能有一天知返，回到[Root.Owner.GetName]包容的怀抱和他们自身注定的命运之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

A misguided faction has seen fit to wound the [Root.Owner.GetName] by removing several slaves from their duties and smuggling them out of [Root.Owner.GetAdj] borders.\n\nWe will heal in time, and we hope that the lost eventually find their way back to the [Root.Owner.GetName] and their purpose in life.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:949`

## faction.55.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.55.title&lang=en)

### 中文

不满的奴隶暴乱！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:867`

### English

Malcontent Slaves Riot!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:956`

## faction.55.desc.colle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.55.desc.colle&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]派系内的某些成员醉心于破坏[Root.Owner.GetName]的长治久安。他们希望通过破坏[riots_planet.GetName]上的生产设施来达到这一目的。我们只能猜想，无论他们造成了什么影响或损害，这一阻碍他们接受[Root.Owner.GetAdj]合法统治的愚蠢行为最后会让他们自取灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

Some elements in the [Root.GetName] faction have been gripped by a desire to hurt the [Root.Owner.GetName] by destroying a productive facility on [riots_planet.GetName]. We can only assume that whatever impairment it is that prevents them from accepting their place in the [Root.Owner.GetAdj] order also drives them to this roundabout form of self-harm.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:957`

## faction.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

暴动的奴隶毁坏了[riots_planet.GetName]上的一栋建筑——要想恢复生产必须先修复建筑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

Rioting slaves have ruined a building on [riots_planet.GetName] - it must be repaired before production can resume.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:958`

## faction.60.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.title&lang=en)

### 中文

不满的奴隶叛乱！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Malcontent Slaves Revolt!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:960`

## faction.60.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶群体中的激进分子——我们并非毫无预料——公然发动武装叛乱反抗我们合法的统治。\n\n据报道称，叛乱在[revolt_planet.GetName]上打响了第一枪，并有可能发展成燎原之势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

The more radical elements among our slave populace have - not unexpectedly, some would say - taken up arms against our lawful rule.\n\nReports indicate that the unrest started on [revolt_planet.GetName], and may already have spread.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:961`

## faction.60.desc.colle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.desc.colle&lang=en)

### 中文

为了[Root.Owner.GetName]的繁荣昌盛，一些人不得不从事比较卑微的工作。而这些人的不满已经酿成了一场灾难。\n\n他们利欲熏心，无法看到大局，竟然在[revolt_planet.GetPlanetMoon][revolt_planet.GetName]上发动了叛乱——而且似乎其它星球也想要跟随他们的步伐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Dissent among those assigned menial tasks for the glory and betterment of the [Root.Owner.GetName] has reached a catastrophic boiling point.\n\nTheir inability to grasp the bigger picture and their place within it has driven them to open rebellion on the [revolt_planet.GetPlanetMoon] [revolt_planet.GetName] - and other worlds may soon follow, if they have not already.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:962`

## faction.60.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是灾难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

Disaster.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:963`

## faction.60.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.b&lang=en)

### 中文

无知的蠢货……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:874`

### English

Ignorant fools...

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:964`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.60.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§H不满奴隶§!派系发动了§Y叛乱§!！

### English

The §HMalcontent Slaves§! faction §Yrevolts§!!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:965`

## faction.80.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.80.title&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶被煽动！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

Slaves Radicalized!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:967`

## faction.80.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.80.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上的一所本地的小礼拜堂成员成功的煽动了几个[Root.Owner.GetAdj]奴隶，不满奴隶派的力量又增强了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

Members of a local [Root.GetName] chapter appear to have successfully radicalized several [Root.Owner.GetAdj] slaves, strengthening the ranks of the malcontent slaves.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:968`

## pft_planet_separatists

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_planet_separatists&lang=en)

### 中文

行星分离主义派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

Planetary Separatist Faction

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:974`

## pft_planet_separatists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apft_planet_separatists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行星分离主义者可能会出现在各个殖民星球里，意在让整个星球脱离帝国统治。如果得到足够的民众支持，他们就有可能发起武装革命。\n\n§H行星分离主义者只会出现在直辖的星球上。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

Planetary separatists may appear on colonized worlds and seek to free the planet from imperial rule. Given enough popular Support, they may even take up arms.\n\n§HPlanetary Separatists will only appear on directly-controlled planets.§!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:975`

## faction.100.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.100.title&lang=en)

### 中文

分离主义者发起反叛！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

Separatists Rebel!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:978`

## faction.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[planet.GetName]上的动乱已经一触即发。当地的行星分离主义派系试图发起一场武装叛乱去夺取行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

The unrest on [planet.GetName] has reached critical mass. The local separatist faction has risen up in an armed attempt to take the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:979`

## faction.101.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.101.title&lang=en)

### 中文

分离主义者发起暴动！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Separatist Riot!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:982`

## faction.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[planet.GetName]§!的内乱程度已经到达白热化阶段，一部分的§Y[Root.GetName]§!成员走上街头打砸抢烧。\n\n报告表明，有殖民地的主要设施被损毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

The civil unrest on §Y[planet.GetName]§! has reached a boiling point and members of the §Y[Root.GetName]§! have taken to the streets, looting and burning.\n\nReports indicate that a major colony facility has been ruined.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:983`

## faction.102.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.102.title&lang=en)

### 中文

派系领袖遇刺！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

Faction Leader Assassinated!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:986`

## faction.102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Leader.GetName]的突然死亡让[Root.GetName]陷入混乱。\n\n在[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]公众看来，这是[Root.GetName]内部政治斗争的结果。虽然派系内的一名新领袖已经逐渐崭露头角，但这一事件也令他们损失了不少的民众支持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is in disarray following the sudden death of their leader, [Root.Leader.GetName].\n\nAs far as the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] public is concerned, this is the outcome of an internal power struggle within the [Root.GetName]. While another figurehead has already emerged, the incident has resulted in a loss of popular support.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:987`

## faction.103.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.103.title&lang=en)

### 中文

建立新星域！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

Sector Created!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:990`

## faction.103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]在[parameter:planet.GetName]建立星域中心政府，并被承认为准自主星域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:894`

### English

The [Root.GetName] has been granted a quasi-independent sector, with local government based on [parameter:planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:991`

## faction.104.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.104.title&lang=en)

### 中文

脱离帝国！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:895`

### English

Empire Released!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:993`

## faction.104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了平息分裂行动并有效肃清分离主义者，已允许[parameter:planet.GetName]的殖民地脱离[Root.Owner.GetName]的统治。\n\n随后[Root.GetName]成立了自己的国家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

To appease and effectively eliminate the separatists, the colony on [parameter:planet.GetName] has been released from the [Root.Owner.GetName].\n\nThe [Root.GetName] have formed their own nation.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:994`

## faction.200.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.200.title&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]剑指异教徒[parameter:heathens.GetName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

[Root.GetName] Brand the Heathen [parameter:heathens.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:999`

## faction.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们将外国势力的首都标记为兴趣点，希望能将其攻占。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

they designate a PoI on a foreign power's capital and want it taken over.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1000`

## holy_war_1_poi_desc

The heathens' planet.

## profane_planets_seized

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprofane_planets_seized&lang=en)

### 中文

异教徒行星的污秽已被洗清！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Profane Planets Cleansed

## faction.201.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.201.title&lang=en)

### 中文

异教徒的首都已攻占！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

Heathen Capital Conquered!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1009`

## faction.201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的神庙已被污染，他们的神已不再眷顾他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

Their temples defaced, their gods have abandoned them.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1010`

## faction.202.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.202.title&lang=en)

### 中文

[parameter:heathens.GetName]受辱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

[parameter:heathens.GetName] Insulted

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1012`

## faction.202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于一个安保方面令人尴尬的失误，[Root.GetName]获得了我们外交沟通频道的基础通信权限。他们借机从自己信仰的角度，对[parameter:heathens.GetName]的各种异端信仰以及信仰产生的所谓道德沦丧进行了谴责和侮辱。\n\n由于[parameter:heathens.GetSpeciesNamePlural]高度宗教化，这一事件显得有些棘手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

As a consequence of an embarrassing lapse in security, the [Root.GetName] gained brief access to our diplomatic communication channels. They used this opportunity to condemn and insult the [parameter:heathens.GetName] for various heresies committed and moral failings inherent in their beliefs.\n\nThe incident did not go over well with the deeply religious [parameter:heathens.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1013`

## faction.202.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.202.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真尴尬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

How embarrassing.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1014`

## faction.202.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.202.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[parameter:enemy.GetName]§!对§Y我们的帝国§!新增了§Y派系羞辱§!评价修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

§Y[parameter:enemy.GetName]§! adds the §YFaction Insult§! opinion modifier towards §Your Empire§!.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1015`

## faction.203.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.203.title&lang=en)

### 中文

羞辱未遂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

Attempted Insult

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1016`

## faction.203.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.203.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于又一个安保方面令人尴尬的失误，[Root.GetName]获得了我们外交沟通频道的基础通信权限。他们再次趁机贬低了[parameter:heathens.GetName]的异端信仰。\n\n然而可以理解的是，[parameter:heathens.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并未被这次重复事件弄得措手不及，只是耸了耸肩无视了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

As a consequence of yet another, intensely embarrassing lapse in security, the [Root.GetName] gained brief access to our diplomatic communication channels. They used this opportunity to once again belittle the heathen beliefs of the [parameter:heathens.GetName].\n\nHowever, the [parameter:heathens.GetSpeciesNamePlural] were understandably not taken off-guard by this repeated incident, and appear to have shrugged it off.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1017`

## faction.203.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.203.a&lang=en)

### English

## faction.204.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.204.title&lang=en)

### 中文

宗教反叛！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

Religious Insurrection!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1020`

## faction.204.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.204.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

情报表示[Root.GetName]的偏激分子正计划在[planet.GetName]发起武装反叛。\n\n这场反叛仍处于初始阶段，我们最好将它消灭在摇篮里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

We have received reports that radical elements of the [Root.GetName] are staging an armed insurrection on [planet.GetName].\n\nTheir rebellion is still in its infancy, and it would be advisable to nip this in the bud.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1021`

### English

## faction.301.title

### 中文

民族主义者蓄意破坏！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### English

Nationalist Sabotage!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1026`

## faction.301.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.301.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的激进分子们摧毁了[planet.GetPlanetMoon]上的一所建筑，位于[planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:922`

### English

Radical members of the [Root.GetName] have ruined a building has on the [planet.GetPlanetMoon] of [planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1027`

## faction.301.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.301.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的一小撮别有用心的异星暴徒已经毁坏了[planet.GetName]一栋建筑，这一行为无疑是对[Root.Owner.GetAdj]利益的蓄意破坏，其目的是为了扰乱我们的社会秩序。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

The xeno brutes of the [Root.GetName] have ruined a building on [planet.GetName] in a calculated attempt to hurt [Root.Owner.GetAdj] interests, with the apparent goal of destroying our way of life.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1028`

## faction.301.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.301.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

看来[Root.GetName]的那些可爱的异见者们十分享受他们表达不满的方式——通过在[planet.GetName]上的蓄意破坏来给我们的政府找点外快来干。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:924`

### English

The otherwise lovely members of the [Root.GetName] have seen fit to express their dissatisfaction with our rule by disabling - some would say sabotaging - an installation on [planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1029`

## faction.302.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.302.title&lang=en)

### 中文

民族主义者反叛！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

Nationalist Rebellion!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1031`

## faction.302.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.302.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

受支持者的鼓舞，[Root.GetName]决定以公然的武力夺回曾属于他们的领土。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

Emboldened by popular support, the [Root.GetName] has taken up arms in an effort to take back their territory by force.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1032`

## faction.302.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.302.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]发动叛乱的异种决定以暴力的手段推翻[Root.Owner.GetAdj]统治！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

The revolting xenos of the [Root.GetName] seek to overthrow [Root.Owner.GetAdj] rule in open rebellion!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1033`

## faction.302.desc.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.302.desc.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

由于我们未能满足对方的要求，[Root.GetName]已经选择公开反叛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

Recognizing our continued failure to accede to their demands, the [Root.GetName] has risen up in open revolt.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1034`

## faction.400.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.400.title&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队遭到破坏！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:930`

### English

Fleet Sabotaged!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1038`

## faction.400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的偏激分子潜入了至少一艘[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]军团舰船并埋下炸药。现在炸药已经引爆，导致[Root.GetName]严重受创。\n\n[From.Leader.GetName]，[From.GetName]的领袖，已发表声明宣称这是令人厌恶的暴力行为和暴徒本质的集中体现，尤其是[Root.Owner.GetName]和[From.parameter:enemy.GetName]的武装冲突。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

At some point, radical members of the [From.GetName] successfully infiltrated at least one [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] military vessel to plant explosives. These explosives have now been detonated, destroying the entirety of the [Root.GetName].\n\n[From.Leader.GetName], leader of the [From.GetName], has issued a statement declaring this action to be demonstrative of the abhorrent nature of violence in general, and the [Root.Owner.GetName]'s armed conflict with the [From.parameter:enemy.GetName] in particular.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1039`

## faction.501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于一个安保方面令人尴尬的失误，[Root.GetName]获得了我们外交沟通频道的基础通信权限。他们趁此机会严重侮辱了[parameter:enemy.GetName]并且要求他们立即归还[parameter:planet.GetName]。\n\n与[parameter:enemy.GetSpeciesNamePlural]沟通的过程不太顺利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:942`

### English

As a consequence of an embarrassing lapse in security, the [Root.GetName] gained brief access to our diplomatic communication channels. They used this opportunity to gravely insult the [parameter:enemy.GetName] and demand the immediate return of [parameter:planet.GetName].\n\nIt did not go over well with the [parameter:enemy.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1054`

They used this opportunity to once again gravely insult the [parameter:enemy.GetName] and demand the immediate return of [parameter:planet.GetName].\n\nHowever, the [parameter:enemy.GetSpeciesNamePlural] were understandably not taken off-guard by this repeated incident, and appear to have shrugged it off.

## faction.600.title

奴隶被煽动

### English

## faction.600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的成员据说已在受到奴役的人群中煽起了异议。据谣传，他们正在训练甚至武装[parameter:slave_faction.GetName]的边缘分子，尽管这一点难以令人置信。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

Members of the [Root.GetName] are allegedly sowing dissent among the enslaved populace. According to some rumors, they are training and even arming fringe elements of the [parameter:slave_faction.GetName], though this seems far too outrageous to be true.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1067`

尽管如此，还是让人担心。

### English

Worrying, nonetheless.

## faction.601.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.601.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有消息指出一些[Root.GetName]的更为激进的成员已经解放了一群[railroad_planet.GetName]上的奴隶，并将他们送到了[Root.Owner.GetName]的控制区之外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

Word has reached up that some of the more radical members of the [Root.GetName] have freed a group of slaves on [railroad_planet.GetName] and spirited them out of the [Root.Owner.GetName]'s reach.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1071`

### English

The [parameter:rebelling_faction.GetName] Rebel!

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1081`

## faction.701.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.701.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据信，由于受够了[Root.Owner.GetName]对生活在身边的粗鄙外星人的软弱立场，[parameter:planet.GetName]上妄图成为奴隶主的一群人已经聚集到一起，结党成社，宣称独立。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:967`

### English

Supposedly fed up with the [Root.Owner.GetName]'s soft stance on the brutish aliens amongst us, the would-be slave owners of [parameter:planet.GetName] have come together, as a confederation of communities, to declare their independence.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1082`

### English

## faction.801.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.801.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[riot_planet.GetName]上受到净化的一群人铤而走险，攻击并摧毁了行星上的一处设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

Fueled by desperation, some individuals currently subject to purges on [riot_planet.GetName] have seen fit to attack and raze one of the facilities on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1087`

## faction.803.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.803.title&lang=en)

[Root.GetName]反叛！

### English

## faction.803.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afaction.803.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[death_planet.GetName]上那些因净化人口而陷入绝望的人向[Root.Owner.GetName]发起了武装反叛，并组建了一支涣散的叛军。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/pop_factions_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

Fueled by desperation, demographics currently subject to purges on [death_planet.GetName] have taken up arms against the [Root.Owner.GetName], forming a ramshackle rebel force.

Source: `localisation/english/pop_factions_l_english.yml:1090`
